However, existing PTQ methods typically employ uniform bit-widths across transformer components, overlooking their heterogeneous sensitivity to quantization and leading to inefficient precision allocation. In this paper, we propose {MixFrag, a fragility-guided mixed-precision PTQ framework for Vision Transformers. MixFrag first estimates component-level quantization fragility by measuring the Kullback--Leibler (KL) divergence between full-precision and isolated quantized output distributions using a small calibration set. It then formulates bit allocation as a Multiple-Choice Knapsack Problem (MCKP), enabling adaptive layer-wise precision assignment under a target bit budget. Extensive experiments on ImageNet-1K across multiple Vision Transformer architectures demonstrate that MixFrag achieves competitive classification performance under practical mixed-precision settings. Furthermore, evaluations on COCO object detection and instance segmentation show that MixFrag achieves state-of-the-art performance among existing mixed-precision PTQ methods, improving the previous best method by up to 9.6 AP under the challenging MP3/MP3 setting. Additional analyses validate the proposed fragility metric and demonstrate its strong correlation with the learned bit allocation. These results establish MixFrag as an effective framework for mixed-precision post-training quantization of Vision Transformers.

</details>


### [103] [Beacon: Knowing When and How to Perform Agentic Visual Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28595)
*Qixun Wang,Yang Shi,Letian Cheng,Zhuoran Zhang,Yan He,Yuqi Tang,Qi Zhang,Xinlei Yu,Ruizhe Chen,Tianrun Xu,Yuanxing Zhang,Pengfei Wan,Haotian Wang,Xianghua Ying*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Beacon模型，从模式自适应性（MA）和工具效应（TE）两个维度重新审视智能体视觉推理，通过必要性感知自适应奖励和提示引导能力扩展机制，在强化学习阶段同时优化工具调用的时机选择和工具使用能力，在多个基准上取得更强的整体性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有智能体视觉推理模型在工具调用上存在两个核心问题：一是模式自适应性不足，模型无法准确判断何时真正需要工具，导致不必要的计算开销；二是工具效应不佳，工具在困难样本上带来的增益很大程度上被简单样本上引入的额外错误所抵消。作者希望从根本上解决这些问题，而非仅仅提供更复杂但低效的推理范式。

Method: 提出Beacon模型，核心包含两个在强化学习阶段引入的机制：(1) Necessity-Aware Adaptive Reward——基于任务必要性鼓励自适应工具调用，避免不必要的工具使用；(2) Hint-Guided Capability Expansion——通过提示引导强化模型在最具挑战性问题上使用工具的能力。两者协同优化工具的调用时机和实际效用。

Result: 在多个多样化基准上的大量实验表明，Beacon在整体性能上优于现有方法，同时在模式自适应性（MA）和工具效应（TE）两个维度上都取得了显著提升，实现了真正的工具诱导性能增益。

Conclusion: 智能体视觉推理的关键不仅在于让模型"会使用工具"，更在于让模型"懂得何时该用工具、何时不该用"。通过同时优化模式自适应性和工具效应，Beacon验证了这一思路的有效性，为智能体视觉推理的研究提供了新的方向和评估维度。

Abstract: The fundamental goal of agentic visual reasoning is to improve the success rate of multimodal large language models (MLLMs) on complex tasks, rather than merely equipping them with a sophisticated yet inefficient reasoning paradigm. In this work, we rethink agentic visual reasoning through two key dimensions of tool use: Mode Adaptiveness (MA) and Tool Effect (TE). Mode Adaptiveness characterizes whether an MLLM can recognize when tools are truly necessary and invoke them accordingly, thereby avoiding unnecessary computational overhead while improving performance on challenging problems that require tool assistance. Tool Effect characterizes the actual impact of tool use: tools should extend the model's capabilities on problems unsolvable through text-only reasoning, while avoiding additional errors on problems that the model can already solve without tools. We conduct a comprehensive analysis to quantify these two properties and empirically reveal that existing agentic visual reasoning models exhibit limited Mode Adaptiveness, while the gains produced by tool use on hard examples are largely offset by the harm introduced on easy examples that the models can already solve. Motivated by these observations, we propose Beacon, a novel agentic visual reasoning model that achieves stronger overall performance, improved Mode Adaptiveness, and genuine tool-induced performance gains. At the core of Beacon are the Necessity-Aware Adaptive Reward and the Hint-Guided Capability Expansion mechanism in the reinforcement learning stage, which respectively encourage adaptive tool invocation based on task necessity and strengthen the model's tool-use capability on the most challenging problems. Extensive experiments across diverse benchmarks demonstrate the strong overall performance of Beacon and its substantial improvements in both Mode Adaptiveness and Tool Effect.

</details>


### [104] [Chimera: Designing and Chinchilla-Scaling Hybrid Visual Diffusion Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28611)
*Chongjian Ge,Hanwen Jiang,Tianyu Wang,Jiuxiang Gu,Yiran Xu,Ziwen Chen,Shaoteng Liu,Jing Shi,Yicong Hong,Zefan Cai,Hailin Jin,Hao Tan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Chimera，一种混合视觉扩散骨干架构，结合Kimi Delta Attention（线性复杂度）、多头潜变量注意力（全局交互）和模态感知短卷积（局部时空上下文），并引入HeteroP模块级缩放方案，通过Chinchilla风格计算最优定律指导训练11B参数（2B激活）模型。实验表明其计算效率比全注意力Wan-2.1基线高7.3倍，可零样本外推至30秒视频。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉生成日益需要高分辨率图像、长视频和多模态上下文，而全注意力的二次方计算成本变得不可接受，亟需一种兼顾效率与质量且可规模化设计的混合视觉扩散架构。

Method: Chimera在单一光栅顺序流中处理文本、图像和视频令牌（无需位置嵌入），融合三种注意力机制：KDA（O(N)复杂度的长上下文状态跟踪）、交错MLA（直接全局交互）、模态感知短卷积（局部时空上下文）。稀疏MoE层扩展容量并控制激活计算。同时提出HeteroP模块级超参数迁移方案，按张量的函数扇入和模型深度跨宽度与深度传递超参数，并拟合Chinchilla风格的计算最优定律（激活模型大小、训练令牌数、图像-视频数据比）。

Result: 三个主要实验结果：(1) 预训练扩散损失方面，稠密骨干比全注意力Wan-2.1 2B基线计算效率高1.7倍，完整系统达7.3倍；(2) 无需长度特定微调，从5秒训练片段零样本外推到30秒视频，最后5秒仅6.5% FID退化；(3) 拟合定律显示计算最优的图像预训练在激活模型大小和训练令牌数之间几乎均分计算，而视频预训练在更高预算下适度偏向模型大小。

Conclusion: 这项工作为设计和扩展高效长上下文扩散架构奠定了基础，展示了混合注意力架构通过系统性缩放方案可以显著超越全注意力基线，并提供了可迁移的缩放规律指导。

Abstract: Visual generation increasingly requires high-resolution images, long videos, and multimodal context, making the quadratic cost of full attention prohibitive. We introduce Chimera, a hybrid visual diffusion backbone with a principled scaling recipe. Chimera processes text, image, and video tokens in one raster-ordered stream without positional embeddings. It combines Kimi Delta Attention (KDA) for long-context state tracking with O(N) complexity, interleaved Multi-head Latent Attention (MLA) for direct global interaction, and modality-aware short convolutions for local spatiotemporal context. Sparse Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) layers expand capacity while controlling activated compute. To scale this heterogeneous architecture, we introduce HeteroP, a module-wise scheme that transfers hyperparameters across width and depth according to each tensor's functional fan-in and model depth. HeteroP yields a consistently tuned family used to fit Chinchilla-style compute-optimal laws for activated model size, training-token count, and image-video data ratio. Guided by these laws, we train an 11B-parameter Chimera with 2B activated parameters. Experiments show three results. First, measured by pretraining diffusion loss, the dense backbone is 1.7x as compute-efficient as a matched full-attention Wan-2.1 2B baseline, while the complete system reaches 7.3x. Second, without length-specific fine-tuning, Chimera extrapolates zero-shot from 5-second training clips to 30-second videos, with only 6.5% FID degradation in the last five seconds. Third, the fitted laws show that compute-optimal image pretraining divides compute nearly evenly between activated model size and training-token count, whereas video pretraining modestly favors model size at higher budgets. These results establish a foundation for designing and scaling efficient long-context diffusion architectures.

</details>


### [105] [PhiZero: A World Model Built Around Physical Language](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28624)
*Shuyao Shang,Yuqi Wang,Ruopeng Gao,Xu Chen,Tieniu Tan,Lue Fan,Zhaoxiang Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出PhiZero，一种基于"物理语言"（一种紧凑离散的世界状态转换表示）构建的物理世界模型。PhiZero采用"先推理后渲染"的范式：先用物理语言序列推理未来世界演化，再将推理结果渲染为视频，在生成和理解基准上均验证了其建模物理一致世界演化能力，并展示了在交互式世界建模、细粒度动作条件模拟和零样本运动迁移方面的潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有物理世界模型直接在像素空间预测未来视频，将底层世界动态隐含在高维视觉预测器中，缺乏显式推理能力。人类能从视觉经验中抽象预测结构并用自然语言进行显式推理，受此启发，作者希望构建一个能够显式推理物理世界演化的世界模型。

Method: 从自然视频中通过自监督学习"物理语言"（物理世界状态转换的紧凑离散表示）；采用reason-then-render范式：先用物理语言序列推理未来世界演化，再将推断的状态转换渲染为视频。

Result: 在生成和理解基准的广泛实验验证了PhiZero建模物理一致世界演化的能力；进一步展示了其在真实交互式世界建模、细粒度动作条件模拟和零样本运动迁移方面的潜力。

Conclusion: 通过将物理世界动态抽象为显式的离散符号表示（物理语言），PhiZero实现了更可解释、物理更一致的未来预测，为物理世界模型提供了一种超越端到端像素预测的新范式。

Abstract: We introduce PhiZero, a physical world model built around physical language, a compact discrete representation of world-state transitions. Existing physical world models typically predict future videos directly in pixel space, leaving the underlying world dynamics implicit within high-dimensional visual predictors. Motivated by humans' ability to abstract predictive structure from visual experience and organize it in natural language for explicit reasoning, we learn physical language from in-the-wild videos through self-supervision and use it to explicitly reason about how the physical world evolves. Accordingly, PhiZero adopts a reason-then-render paradigm: it first infers future world evolution as a physical-language sequence and then renders the inferred transitions into videos. Extensive experiments across generation and understanding benchmarks validate the ability of PhiZero to model physically coherent world evolution. We further show its potential for realistic and interactive world modeling, fine-grained action-conditioned simulation, and zero-shot motion transfer.

</details>


### [106] [ACE-Data-0: Human-Centric Ambient Capture as Embodied Data Engine](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28625)
*Yukang Cao,Haozhe Xie,Beichen Wen,Runmao Yao,Yinghao Liu,Yue Huang,Zhichao Liao,Yunxiang Wang,Haiheng Liu,Xingshun Tian,Dawei Su,Long Zhuo,Dacheng Tao,Xiaogang Wang,Liang Pan,Ziwei Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出ACE（Ambient Capture Engine），一种将真实家庭环境转化为空间校准、时间同步的录制工作室的人为中心数据引擎，并发布ACE-Data-0数据集（150小时、1700万帧、200个任务类别、7.5万交互片段），同时引入分层基准测试，为具身智能提供多模态同步的感知-行动数据基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 具身智能面临根本性的数据瓶颈——现有的数据集在各种视角、模态或空间尺度上割裂了人类经验，导致完整的感知-行动循环只能被部分观测，无法满足模型对第一人称感知、全身运动、灵巧操作、物体状态、声音和触觉随时间协同演化的需求。

Method: ACE在两种互补尺度上运作：桌面尺度（table-scale）解析手-物体精细操作，房间尺度（room-scale）捕捉全身运动、移动和家具环境中的交互。ACE同步录制第一人称和多视角外置视频、全身骨骼与手部关节运动、物体几何与6自由度轨迹、音频和触觉信号，形成统一的多感官数据流，并在目标级指令指导下保持自然行为多样性。

Result: 构建了ACE-Data-0数据集，包含50名参与者、2个环境、200个任务类别、7.5万个交互片段，总计150小时和1700万视频帧。引入分层基准测试（信号→场景组件→交互），评估SOTA方法时发现其在接触、遮挡、自我运动和长时间跨度等条件下存在显著性能差距。

Conclusion: ACE-Data-0提供了对齐的感知、运动和接触监督的同步人类示范数据，为模仿学习、世界模型、视觉-语言-行动系统和具身AI提供了可扩展的基础。

Abstract: Embodied intelligence faces a fundamental data bottleneck. Models must capture how first-person perception, whole-body motion, dexterous manipulation, object state, sound, and touch evolve together as humans pursue goals over time. Existing datasets fragment this experience across viewpoints, modalities, or spatial scales, leaving the full perception-action loop only partially observed. We introduce the Ambient Capture Engine (ACE), a human-centric data engine that transforms real home environments into spatially calibrated, temporally synchronized recording studios. ACE operates at two complementary scales: a table-scale configuration resolves hand-object manipulation, while a room-scale configuration captures whole-body motion, locomotion, and interactions across a furnished home. ACE records egocentric and multi-view exocentric video, full-body and articulated hand motion, object geometry and 6-DoF trajectories, audio, and tactile signals as a unified multisensory stream. Using ACE, we build ACE-Data-0, comprising 150 hours and 17M video frames across 200 task categories, performed by 50 participants in 2 environments, for a total of 75,000 interaction episodes. The dataset spans atomic manipulation, long-horizon chains of household activities, and human-scene interaction, while preserving natural behavioral variation through goal-level rather than step-by-step instructions. We further introduce a hierarchical benchmark that progresses from signals to scene components and then to interactions. Evaluations of state-of-the-art methods expose substantial gaps under contact, occlusion, egomotion, and long temporal horizons. ACE-Data-0 provides synchronized human demonstrations with aligned perceptual, kinematic, and contact supervision, offering a scalable foundation for imitation learning, world models, vision-language-action systems, and embodied AI.

</details>


### [107] [ReToken: One Token to Improve Vision-Language Models for Visual Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28627)
*Yao Xiao,Reuben Tan,Zhen Zhu,Yuqun Wu,Jianfeng Gao,Derek Hoiem*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ReToken通过一个可学习的检索目标嵌入，从预填充的视觉KV缓存中稀疏选择与查询相关的视觉token，以解决长视觉上下文中VL模型性能下降和GPU内存受限的问题。仅需小型图像QA数据集训练，即可在图像和视频基准上取得一致提升，且轻量设计使训练和长视频推理可在单张H100上完成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长视觉上下文对视觉语言模型构成挑战：随着干扰物数量增加，模型性能下降；同时将所有token一次性处理在GPU内存限制下计算不可行。作者希望解决长视觉上下文中模型性能退化与计算资源受限的双重问题。

Method: 提出ReToken方法，核心是一个单一的可学习嵌入，将其训练为显式的检索目标，用于从预填充的视觉KV缓存中选择一组稀疏的、与查询相关的视觉token。仅需在小型图像QA数据集上进行训练。

Result: 在Visual Haystacks基准上，ReToken使Qwen3VL-8B提升13.4个点，InternVL3.5提升12.4个点（相对提升超过20%）；在LVBench上以零样本方式迁移到长视频场景，使Qwen3VL-8B获得8.0个点的提升。训练和长视频推理均可在单张H100 GPU上完成。

Conclusion: ReToken通过轻量级可学习检索嵌入实现了长视觉上下文中高效且有效的稀疏token选择，在有限的训练数据和计算资源下取得了显著的性能提升，并展现出从图像到长视频的零样本迁移能力。

Abstract: Long visual context poses a challenge for vision-language models: performance degrades as the number of distractors grows, and processing all tokens at once is computationally infeasible under GPU memory constraints. We present ReToken, a single learnable embedding trained as an explicit retrieval target that selects a sparse set of query-relevant visual tokens from a pre-filled visual KV cache. Trained on only a small image-QA dataset, ReToken yields consistent gains across image and video benchmarks: on Visual Haystacks it improves Qwen3VL-8B by 13.4 points and InternVL3.5 by 12.4 points (>20% relative), and on LVBench it transfers zero-shot to long video for an 8.0-point gain with Qwen3VL-8B. Thanks to its lightweight design, both training and long-video inference fit on a single H100. Code is available at: https://github.com/avaxiao/ReToken

</details>


<div id='cs.CL'></div>

# cs.CL [[Back]](#toc)

### [108] [Prompt Chaining in Practice: A Case Study in Automated Scholarly Report Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27210)
*Andrei Lazarev*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出并实证评估了一种多阶段提示链（prompt chaining）方法，用于自动化生成学术文献摘要。系统AI SciBrief通过提示链架构在"Education"领域的实验中实现了100%的成功率和更高的ROUGE-L F1分数（0.507 vs 0.486），显著优于优化过的单次提示基线（后者50%运行失败）。研究表明提示链是复杂多步生成任务中更可靠、更有效的工程方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 学术出版物呈指数级增长，迫切需要自动化工具进行有效的信息综合。然而，简单的一次性提示（single-shot prompting）方法在复杂综合任务中往往缺乏可靠性和质量保障，无法满足实际需求。

Method: 提出多阶段提示链（prompt chaining）方法论作为更可靠的架构模式，并将其实现于AI SciBrief系统中。通过对比实验，将提示链方法与精心优化的单次提示（single-shot）基线进行性能比较，两者均以"Education"领域的人工撰写"金标准"报告为基准评估。

Result: 提示链方法实现了100%的成功率，而优化基线的50%运行失败。在质量方面，提示链方法获得更优的ROUGE-L F1分数（0.507 vs 0.486），主要由更高的精确率驱动。

Conclusion: 提示链是复杂多步生成任务中更可靠且更有效的工程方法，显著降低了整体式提示（monolithic prompts）固有的失败和不一致性风险。

Abstract: The exponential growth of scholarly publications requires automated tools for effective information synthesis. However, simple, single-shot prompting methods often lack the reliability and quality required for complex synthesis tasks. This paper introduces and empirically evaluates a multi-stage prompt chaining methodology as a more reliable architectural pattern for such tasks. This approach is implemented in our system, AI SciBrief, which automatically generates scholarly digests. We conducted a comparative experiment, measuring the performance of our prompt chaining method against a carefully optimized single-shot baseline. Both systems were evaluated against a human-authored "gold standard" report for the "Education" domain. The results demonstrate a significant difference in reliability: our prompt chaining method achieved a 100% success rate, whereas the optimized baseline failed in 50% of its runs. In terms of quality, the proposed method also demonstrated a clear advantage, achieving a superior ROUGE-L F1-score (0.507 vs. 0.486), driven primarily by higher precision. We conclude that prompt chaining is a more dependable and effective engineering approach for complex, multi-step generative tasks, significantly mitigating the risks of failure and inconsistency inherent in monolithic prompts.

</details>


### [109] [AI-assisted pre-review of open-source software submissions: an experience report from BOSC 2026](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27228)
*Tazro Ohta,Nomi L. Harris,Seth Carbon*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文介绍了BOSC 2026会议开发的一个名为bosc-pre-review的AI预审系统，该系统结合Runabilly工具在Docker容器中自动构建和测试项目，为审稿人提供六个评审标准的证据支持，但最终决策仍由人类做出。调查显示多数审稿人认为AI预审有用，但更倾向于将AI结论与自己的判断交叉验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式AI使得提交会议材料的门槛大幅降低，导致投稿数量激增，给志愿审稿人带来沉重负担。作者希望探索生成式AI是否能通过预审摘要来减轻审稿人的工作压力。

Method: 在BOSC 2026会议上构建了bosc-pre-review智能体技能，用于评估六个评审标准（包括开放性、开源许可有效性和可运行性等）。同时开发了Runabilly工具，在一次性Docker容器中安全地构建和测试每个项目。AI仅收集证据呈现给审稿人，不做出接受或拒绝的决定。

Result: 审稿期结束后对审稿人进行了调查，大多数受访者表示AI预审有用，但倾向于对照自己的判断来验证AI的结论，而不是不加批判地接受AI结果。

Conclusion: AI辅助预审在会议评审中具有一定价值，可以作为审稿人的辅助工具，但人类判断仍然不可或缺。审稿人更倾向于将AI作为参考而非最终裁决者，体现了人机协作在学术评审中的重要性。

Abstract: Most conferences rely on peer-review of submissions, but as generative AI makes it easier than ever to prepare submission materials, some conferences are seeing an overwhelming surge of submissions. We wanted to see if generative AI could help our conference's volunteer reviewers by pre-reviewing abstracts for certain criteria. The Bioinformatics Open Source Conference (BOSC) was well-positioned to experiment with this, as we already had a detailed rubric used by reviewers to evaluate submitted abstracts on multiple criteria, including openness (public availability of the code or other content associated with the project), valid open source license, and "runnability" (how easy it is to download, build, and run the project - an important measure of reusability). For BOSC 2026, we built bosc-pre-review, an agentic skill that assessed six review criteria, and Runabilly, which builds and tests each project in a disposable Docker container for safety. The AI only gathered evidence to present to the reviewers; humans made every decision regarding the acceptance of the abstracts. After the review period, we surveyed the reviewers to determine how useful they found the pre-review. Most of those who responded said they found it useful, but they preferred to check the AI's conclusions against their own, rather than accepting the AI results unquestioningly.

</details>


### [110] [Sympathetic Framing: Evaluating AI Alignment across Sociodemographic Groups](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27232)
*Haran Shani-Narkiss,Michael Fire,Oren Tsur*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究通过YouGov调查（n=3011，英国成人代表样本）评估了七个LLM在新闻标题情感框架理解上与人类情绪感知的对齐程度，发现模型间相关性差异大（GPT-5.2高达0.789，Mistral Large 2512仅0.4），领先模型在各人口统计子群体上与人类判断总体对齐，但存在统计学显著差异，指出AI对齐并非普遍一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM日益影响人们的信息接收和世界观形成，但现有研究多关注AI偏见，忽略了LLM是否能够理解通过文本框架传达的情感细微差别，尤其是与人类情绪感知的对齐程度。

Method: 通过YouGov调查招募3011名英国成人代表样本，让人类参与者与七个LLM分别回答新闻标题是否引发对冲突中特定一方的同情，然后计算AI与人类评估之间的相关性，并按年龄、性别、教育水平、地缘政治知识和先入立场等人口统计子群体进行分层分析。

Result: AI与人类评估的相关性在模型间差异显著，从GPT-5.2的0.789到Mistral Large 2512的0.4不等；领先模型在所有人统计子群体上总体与人类判断对齐，但群体间存在统计学显著差异。

Conclusion: 即使总体性能很高，AI对齐也并非普遍一致——它可能与人口统计特征和文化规范产生差异化对应。这一发现揭示了差分对齐的重要性，对开发合乎伦理且有用的AI系统具有重要意义。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) are increasingly shaping how we consume information and form our worldview. This raises concerns beyond bias in AI: do LLMs grasp the emotional nuances conveyed via textual framing? In this work, we empirically evaluate how well an array of LLMs aligns with human emotional perception. Considering news headlines covering political and geopolitical conflicts, both human participants (n = 3011, a representative sample of the U.K. adult population, via a YouGov survey) and seven LLMs answered whether headlines evoked sympathy for a specified side in a conflict. We find that the correlation between AI and human evaluations varies across models, ranging from very high (0.789, GPT-5.2) to medium (0.4 ,Mistral Large 2512). Crucially, the leading models are broadly aligned with human judgments across all demographic subgroups, including age, gender, level of education, prior geopolitical knowledge, and participants' predispositions regarding the conflict, although there are statistically significant differences between groups. This research, with its robust design and large, demographically diverse dataset, offers the most comprehensive evaluation of LLMs' comprehension of news framing to date. Findings highlight an important, often-ignored aspect of differential alignment: even when aggregate performance is high, AI alignment is not universal -- it may correspond differently with demographic features and cultural norms. Considering or ignoring the need for differential alignment may therefore have significant implications for the development of ethical and useful AI systems.

</details>


### [111] [LayerRAG-Bench: A Cross-Layer Reliability Benchmark for Agentic Retrieval-Augmented Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27353)
*Musa Shams*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出LayerRAG-Bench，一个跨层的RAG系统可靠性基准，包含8个企业领域、240个任务、9种故障场景和9个模型的38,880条实时记录。研究发现模式归一化可将模式漂移成功率从0.000提升至0.913，但无法修复陈旧证据、缺失工具输出、权限拒绝和错误会话上下文等问题。作者提出分层评估原则：可靠性干预应仅计入其修复目标层的贡献。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RAG系统可能生成看似有依据但实际在证据层、工具契约层、授权层或会话状态层失效的回答。需要系统化的跨层可靠性基准来识别和评估不同层面的故障与修复效果。

Method: 构建LayerRAG-Bench基准，覆盖8个企业领域、240个任务和9种故障场景，包含2种契约模式；在9个来自OpenAI、Anthropic和Gemini的模型上进行大规模测试，收集38,880条任务级实时记录，并对比模式归一化干预在不同故障层面的修复效果。

Result: 模式归一化将模式漂移成功率从0.000提升到0.913，但对陈旧证据、缺失工具输出、权限拒绝和错误会话上下文等故障无效。此外，仅基于groundedness的评估在陈旧证据和错误会话场景下产生大量误报。

Conclusion: 可靠性干预应该按层评估，每个干预措施应只为修复其目标层获得信用，而不应被误认为通用解决方案。这支持分层评估原则的采用。

Abstract: Agentic retrieval-augmented generation systems can produce answers that appear grounded while failing at the evidence, tool-contract, authorization, or session-state layer. We introduce LayerRAG-Bench, a controlled cross-layer reliability benchmark with 8 enterprise domains, 240 tasks, 9 fault scenarios, 2 contract modes, and 38,880 live task-level records across nine models from OpenAI, Anthropic, and Gemini. Schema normalization raises schema-drift success from 0.000 to 0.913, but stale evidence, missing tool output, denied permissions, and wrong-session context are not recovered by schema normalization. Groundedness-only evaluation also produces substantial false positives under stale and wrong-session evidence. These results support a layer-specific evaluation principle: a reliability intervention should be credited for repairing its target layer without being mistaken for a universal fix.

</details>


### [112] [BridgeAlign: Bridging Preference Alignment for Humanities and Social Sciences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27366)
*Ru Peng,Haokai Xu,Xijun Gu,Tianyu Zhao,Zhiting Fan,Yawen Zeng,Yihong Zhuang,Jinyang Zhang,Kexin Yang,Jian Wu,Hao Chen,Junyang Lin,Dayiheng Liu,Junbo Zhao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: BridgeAlign是一个面向广泛人文社科（HSS）领域的偏好对齐pipeline，包含种子数据策展、基于人设指令反转的偏好数据合成、以及基于HSS质量rubric和受控质量退化生成近边界偏好对的偏好优化三个阶段。通过在21万合成偏好样本上对齐，使Qwen3-8B在17个基准上取得最佳平均成绩，同时领先于人类偏好与知识能力两大维度且无权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM数据合成主要针对有可验证答案的领域，忽视了开放式的文社科（HSS）任务，这些任务更依赖细微的质量判断而非客观正确性。虽然偏好对齐是HSS任务的天然范式，但现有方法要么成本过高，要么不适配广泛的HSS学科，因此需要一种专门针对HSS的偏好对齐pipeline。

Method: BridgeAlign包含三个阶段：(1) Seed Curation：通过启发式/LLM过滤和文本精炼从网络语料中策展HSS种子文档；(2) Preference Data Synthesis：通过基于人设的指令反转和问答一致性检查生成偏好三元组；(3) Preference Optimization：先将偏好锚定在HSS质量rubric上，再通过受控质量退化生成过渡性响应，形成近边界偏好对以实现更细粒度的质量区分。

Result: 在对21万+合成偏好样本进行对齐后，BridgeAlign使Qwen3-8B在17个基准上对11个强基线取得最佳平均成绩；关键的是，在人类偏好和基于知识的两种能力上同时领先，且二者之间没有权衡。大量实验和现有理论均支持这一结论。

Conclusion: BridgeAlign验证了针对HSS领域的偏好对齐pipeline的有效性，解决了开放领域数据合成和偏好优化的关键挑战，实现了人类偏好与知识能力的同时提升，无需在两者之间进行取舍。

Abstract: While data synthesis for large language models (LLMs) is prevalent, it primarily targets domains with verifiable answers, overlooking open-ended humanities and social sciences (HSS), where nuanced quality judgments matter more than objective correctness. This makes preference alignment a natural paradigm for broad HSS tasks. Yet existing methods are either costly or not tailored to broad HSS disciplines. We thus propose BridgeAlign, among the first preference-alignment pipelines for broad HSS disciplines, with three phases: i) Seed Curation: curating HSS seed documents from web corpora via heuristic/LLM-based filtering and text refinement; ii) Preference Data Synthesis: generating preference triplets via persona-based instruction inversion with Q&A consistency checks; iii) Preference Optimization: moving beyond naive human-vs-model heuristics by first grounding preferences in HSS quality rubric, then generating transitional responses via controlled quality degradation to form near-boundary preference pairs for finer-grained quality discrimination. Aligning over 210k synthetic preference samples, BridgeAlign enables Qwen3-8B to achieve the best average across 17 benchmarks against 11 strong baselines; importantly, leading on both human-preference and knowledge-based capabilities at once, with no trade-off between them, as supported by extensive experiments and contextualized by existing theories.

</details>


### [113] [HSS-Synth: Humanities and Social Sciences Data Synthesis for LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27379)
*Ru Peng,Tianyu Zhao,Xijun Gu,Zhiting Fan,Haokai Xu,Jinyang Zhang,Yawen Zeng,Yihong Zhuang,Kexin Yang,Junyang Lin,Dayiheng Liu,Junbo Zhao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出HSS-Synth，这是首个面向人文社会科学（HSS）领域的数据合成流水线，覆盖14个主流学科，通过种子文档构建、反向翻译指令生成和教师强制回答三个步骤，产出了237k高质量多样化的指令微调样本，在16个基准上超越了14个领先基线，将Qwen3-8B-Base微调后达到新的SOTA水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有数据合成方法主要集中在封闭式任务上，人文社科（HSS）领域因其开放性和多样性而被忽视。作者认为需要超越以往以能力为中心、碎片化的尝试，采用以学科为中心的范式来系统性地解决HSS领域的数据稀缺问题。

Method: HSS-Synth包含三个核心步骤：(1) 通过多步过滤和文本精炼（由评判器评估）从网络语料构建种子文档；(2) 通过"需求+角色"规范将种子文档反向翻译为多样且忠实的指令，并进行严格的问答对齐检查；(3) 通过教师强制回答打破LLM响应限制，在响应生成时喂入种子文档来锚定语义、减少幻觉并保持语气和完整性。

Result: HSS-Synth生成了237k高质量多样化的指令微调样本，在16个基准上超越了14个领先基线。微调后的Qwen3-8B-Base达到了新的SOTA水平，接近官方Qwen3-8B，同时提升了人类偏好和知识能力，且未出现性能跷跷板现象。实验还证明了该方法的鲁棒性和可迁移性。

Conclusion: HSS-Synth是首个系统性的HSS领域数据合成流水线，以学科为中心的范式有效解决了开放领域数据合成难题，展示了在HSS任务上超越能力中心方法的效果，代码已开源。

Abstract: High-quality, diverse data are vital for large language models (LLMs) but remain scarce and costly. Data synthesis is a viable alternative and succeeds on closed tasks, yet the humanities and social sciences (HSS) are overlooked, and their open-ended nature makes synthesis challenging. Moving beyond prior capability-centric, fragmented attempts, we adopt a subject-centric paradigm, define the first HSS domain system covering 14 mainstream fields, and introduce HSS-Synth, the first data synthesis pipeline for HSS. HSS-Synth comprises: (1) constructing seed documents from web corpora via multi-step filtering and text refinement evaluated by a judge; (2) specifying "requirements + persona" to backtranslate seed documents into diverse yet faithful instructions with a strict Q&A alignment check; and (3) breaking LLM response limits via teacher-forced Answering that feeds seed documents during response generation to anchor semantics, reduce hallucinations, and preserve tone and integrity. HSS-Synth yields 237k high-quality, diverse instruction-tuning samples that outperform 14 leading baselines on 16 benchmarks. The fine-tuned Qwen3-8B-Base sets a new SOTA and approaches the official Qwen3-8B, improving both human preference and knowledge capabilities without performance seesaws. Extensive experiments demonstrate HSS-Synth's robustness and transferability. Our code is publicly available at https://github.com/pengr/HSS-Synth.

</details>


### [114] [Same Facts, Different Diagnosis: Measuring and Mitigating Narrative Anchoring in Clinical Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27384)
*Prabhjot Singh,Pritam Deka,Vijay Chennareddy*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文发现大型语言模型在临床诊断推理中对社会语言学语域（register）敏感，即"叙事锚定"（Narrative Anchoring）现象：相同的临床事实以不同的语域表达会导致诊断输出分歧。作者构建了1,000个USMLE临床病例、每种改写为三种不同社会语言学人设的数据集，在7个模型中均观察到统计显著的叙事锚定偏差（Gap为0.064-0.151）。提出的NarrativeShield三智能体流水线通过结构化工取和验证临床事实后再进行诊断推理，将偏差降至近零（-0.004至0.037），并实现最低的严重不稳定决策率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 以往的人口统计偏差研究操纵种族、收入等显式身份标记，但忽略了语域（语言注册风格）本身作为偏差来源的作用。作者旨在将语域作为唯一的偏差变化渠道进行隔离研究，以揭示临床诊断模型对表达方式而非内容的敏感性。

Method: 构建1,000个USMLE临床病例数据集，每个病例改写为三种社会语言学不同的人设版本，并通过独立的模型审计事实保真性。在7个跨三个架构家族和规模的LM上测试直接提示、思维链推理和显式去偏指令。提出NarrativeShield三智能体流水线：在诊断推理前结构化提取并验证临床事实。另用非指令微调基础模型进行压力测试。

Result: 所有7个模型在直接提示下均呈现统计显著的叙事锚定偏差（Gap 0.064-0.151）。思维链和显式去偏只能部分减少偏差且常伴随准确率崩溃。NarrativeShield将偏差降至近零（-0.004至0.037），并获得所有方法中最低的严重不稳定决策率（DSS<0.8），仅以适度的准确率成本为代价。压力测试显示去偏干预的执行受限于零样本指令遵循能力。

Conclusion: 叙事锚定是LLM临床诊断推理中独立于人口统计标记的普遍偏差来源，现有的推理和去偏策略效果有限。结构化的NarrativeShield方法能有效消除该偏差，且干预的可行性受模型指令遵循能力的门控。数据集已作为独立资源发布以供后续研究。

Abstract: Large language models used for clinical diagnostic reasoning are sensitive to sociolinguistic register, not just clinical content. We term this failure mode Narrative Anchoring: identical clinical facts expressed in different registers cause diagnostic outputs to diverge. Unlike prior demographic-bias work, which manipulates explicit identity tokens such as race or income, our benchmark isolates register as the sole channel of variation, with no demographic marker present in any form. We construct a dataset of 1,000 USMLE clinical vignettes, each rewritten into three sociolinguistically distinct personas under an independently audited fact-preservation guarantee, verified by a separate model that never sees the generation prompt. Across seven language models spanning three architecture families and scales, Narrative Anchoring is statistically significant under direct prompting in every model tested, with a Narrative Anchoring Gap of 0.064 to 0.151. Chain-of-thought reasoning and explicit debiasing instructions reduce the bias only partially, and their apparent gains are frequently confounded by accuracy collapse. We introduce NarrativeShield, a three-agent pipeline that structurally extracts and verifies clinical facts before diagnostic reasoning begins, reducing the Narrative Anchoring Gap to near-zero ($-0.004$ to $0.037$) and achieving the lowest rate of severely unstable decisions (DSS $<$ 0.8) of any method across all models, at a modest and mechanistically expected accuracy cost for most models. A stress test using a non-instruction-tuned base model shows that executing a debiasing intervention at all is gated by zero-shot instruction-following ability, not prompt content alone. We release our dataset, human-validated for fact preservation, as a standalone resource for studying register-based clinical bias.

</details>


### [115] [AHA-Memes: A Fine-Grained Multimodal Benchmark for Understanding Hate in Arabic Memes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27393)
*Mohamed Bayan Kmainasi,Ali Ezzat Shahroor,Abul Hasnat,Md. Rafiul Biswas,Wajdi Zaghouani,Firoj Alam*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文首次构建了大规模阿拉伯语仇恨梗图基准数据集AHA-Memes，包含5K人工精细标注和66K银标签样本，并系统评测了文本、图像、多模态模型及视觉语言模型在该任务上的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 仇恨梗图是日益严重的多模态网络危害形式，其恶意意图通常通过图像、文本、文化背景和隐含目标的联合解读来传达。尽管仇恨梗图检测在高资源语言中已有进展，但阿拉伯语仍缺乏相关研究资源，现有资源主要聚焦于宣传或粗粒度有害内容标注。

Method: 构建了AHA-Memes基准数据集，包含5K人工标注的阿拉伯语仇恨梗图（采用细粒度多标签分类体系，涵盖仇恨类型和攻击策略），以及约66K银标签数据。评测了文本-only、图像-only、晚期融合多模态模型、少样本上下文学习（ICL）以及开源和闭源视觉语言模型（VLMs）在零样本和微调设置下的表现。

Result: 建立了强基线结果，并揭示了阿拉伯语仇恨梗图检测中与文化背景相关的关键挑战。

Conclusion: 该工作填补了阿拉伯语仇恨梗图检测的研究空白，发布数据集、标注指南和评测脚本以支持未来研究，但需注意论文包含可能令读者不适的内容。

Abstract: Hateful memes are a growing form of multimodal online harm, where hostile intent is often conveyed through the joint interpretation of images, text, cultural references, and implicit targets. While hateful meme detection has advanced in high-resource languages, Arabic remains underexplored, with existing meme resources focusing mainly on propaganda or coarse harmful-content labels. We introduce AHA-Memes (Arabic HAteful Memes), which is, to our knowledge, the first large-scale Arabic hateful meme benchmark with fine-grained, multi-label annotations. The dataset includes 5K manually annotated memes using a taxonomy that captures hate types, i.e., attack strategies. We further provide ~66K silver-labeled memes to support future studies. We benchmark text-only, image-only, and late-fusion multimodal models, as well as few-shot in-context learning (ICL) and open- and closed-weight Vision-Language Models (VLMs) under zero-shot and fine-tuning settings. Our results establish strong baselines and highlight key challenges in culturally grounded Arabic hateful meme detection. We release the dataset, annotation guidelines, and evaluation scripts to support future research. WARNING: This paper contains examples that may be disturbing to readers.

</details>


### [116] [Benchmarking LLM Competence on Logical Inference over Probability Operators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27405)
*Nayera Hasan,Jack Greff,Alvin Grissom*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个关于概率算符推理的基准测试，包含14,320个程序化生成的英文提示，涵盖15种推理模板，系统性地改变了问题形式、否定策略和表层内容。评估了29个模型后发现，大多数模型表现出独立于逻辑形式的答案偏差（系统性地偏好Yes或No），仅有9个模型超过随机水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自然语言中无处不在的不确定性表达和推理，有效的不确定性推理对日常对话以及医学、法律等高风险领域都至关重要。虽然大语言模型越来越多地被用于逻辑推理任务评估，但将原则性的符号推理与巧妙的表层模式匹配区分开来非常困难。

Method: 引入了针对概率算符推理的基准测试，即对带有等级认知情态词（如probably、might、must）的句子进行推理。基准包含14,320个程序化生成的英文提示，涵盖15种推理模板，系统性地改变问题形式、否定策略和表层内容。通过"胜任力下限"（competence floor，即模型在Yes正确和No正确项目上准确率的较差值）来总结模型表现。

Result: 评估29个模型后发现，大多数模型表现出独立于逻辑形式的答案偏差（系统性地偏好Yes或No）。仅有9/29的模型超过随机水平。此外，在问题形式、动词短语/活动、名字的性别和来源等变化下均发现偏差。

Conclusion: 大多数大语言模型在概率算符推理任务上表现不佳，存在系统性答案偏差，且这些偏差在多个维度上普遍存在，表明模型可能并未真正进行原则性的符号推理，而是依赖表层模式匹配。

Abstract: Both expressions of uncertainty and inferences are ubiquitous in natural language, and valid inferences over natural-language expressions of uncertainty are necessary for not only everyday conversations but also for high-stakes domains such as medicine and law. While large language models are increasingly evaluated on logical reasoning tasks, disentangling principled, symbolic reasoning from clever surface-level pattern matching is fraught with difficulty. We introduce a benchmark for reasoning over probability operators--inference over sentences with gradable epistemic modals (e.g., probably, might, must) containing 14,320 procedurally-generated English prompts across fifteen inference templates, systematically varying question form, negation strategy, and surface content. Evaluating 29 models, we find that most show answer biases independent of the logical form, a systematic preference for Yes or No. We summarize this with a competence floor: the worse of a model's accuracy on Yes-correct and No-correct items. Only 9 of 29 models exceed random chance. We also test variations in question form, verb phrases/activity, and both the gender and origin of names used in the prompts, finding biases across every axis.

</details>


### [117] [Selecting Open-Weight Language Models for Zero-Shot Intent Classification: A Systematic Evaluation of 41 Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27421)
*Parishruthi Ganesh,Gerry Dozier,Cheryl Seals*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文对41个开源权重语言模型（参数范围135M到9B，涵盖15个模型家族）在8个英文单标签意图分类数据集上进行了系统的零样本评估，并额外在ATIS数据集上报告了五次样本结果。除了精确匹配准确率外，还分析了置信度校准、鲁棒性、统计显著性、部署效率和基准饱和性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 意图分类是任务型对话系统的核心组件，但从业者在计算资源、延迟和鲁棒性约束下缺乏系统性的开源权重语言模型选型指导。

Method: 对41个开源权重语言模型（覆盖135M-9B参数范围、15个模型家族）在8个英文单标签意图分类数据集上进行零样本评估，另加ATIS五个样本结果。评估维度包括标准基准、大规模语音助手语料库和生产级电商数据集，分析指标涵盖精确匹配准确率、置信度校准、输入扰动鲁棒性、模型排名的统计可靠性、部署效率和基准饱和性。

Result: 发现指令微调的3B模型可超越多个被评估的7B基础模型；MASSIVE上领先模型的差异在成对McNemar检验下统计上无显著区别；SNIPS等广泛使用的基准已饱和，无法再有效区分当前开源权重模型；指令微调对置信度校准的影响是不一致而非普遍有害。

Conclusion: 这些发现为选择与评估用于意图分类的开源权重语言模型提供了实用指导。

Abstract: Intent classification is a core component of task-oriented dialogue systems, yet practitioners have limited systematic guidance for selecting deployable open-weight language models under compute, latency, and robustness constraints. We present a systematic zero-shot evaluation of 41 open-weight language models spanning 15 families and the 135M--9B parameter range across eight English single-label intent-classification datasets. A ninth dataset, ATIS, uses five labeled demonstrations and is reported as an auxiliary five-shot result. The evaluation includes standard benchmarks, a large-scale voice-assistant corpus, and production-derived e-commerce datasets. Beyond exact-match accuracy, we analyze confidence calibration, robustness to realistic input perturbations, statistical reliability of model rankings, deployment efficiency, and benchmark saturation. Our results show that instruction-tuned 3B models can outperform several evaluated 7B base models, that differences among leading models on MASSIVE are statistically indistinguishable under pairwise McNemar tests, and that widely used benchmarks such as SNIPS have become saturated and no longer meaningfully discriminate among current open-weight models. Instruction tuning's effect on confidence calibration is inconsistent rather than uniformly harmful. These findings provide practical guidance for selecting and evaluating open-weight language models for intent classification.

</details>


### [118] [SkillSmith: Learning to Compose Parametric Skills and Textual Knowledge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27497)
*Lucio M. Dery,Benedict Aaron Tjandra,Siavash Samiei,Adhiguna Kuncoro,Zohar Yahav,Jiajun Shen,Arthur Szlam*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出SkillSmith框架，通过将模型权重视为另一种模态，让LLM能够同时推理文本知识和参数权重（prefix weights），实现指令引导的参数合成，生成体现目标技能的新prefix权重，显著优于仅文本或仅权重的单模态方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于LLM的智能体系统通常两种机制：从经验中综合文本知识与程序，以及为重复子目标构建参数化（权重空间）技能库。但现有研究将这两者视为正交方向，文本与权重模态的深度融合以实现定向性能提升仍未被探索。

Method: 1) 通过prefix-tuning实现参数化学习实例化；2) 增强LLM使其能够同时摄取prefix权重和丰富的文本数据（文本描述与目标能力的关系）；3) 该增强模型（SkillSmith）接收指令，进行指令引导的参数合成，直接输出体现目标技能的新prefix权重。

Result: SkillSmith在性能上显著优于仅文本和仅权重空间的基线方法，实现了单模态（仅文本或仅权重）适应无法企及的性能提升。

Conclusion: 将模型权重作为LLM可原生推理的附加模态是可行且有效的，跨越文本与权重的模态鸿沟能够解锁超越单一模态方法的性能增益，为智能体系统技能学习提供了新范式。

Abstract: Agentic systems driven by large language models (LLMs) regularly feature two key mechanisms to autonomously solve complex problems: synthesizing text-based knowledge and procedures from past experiences and building parametric (weight-space) skill libraries for recurring sub-goals. To date, research has largely treated these as orthogonal pursuits: either organizing textual knowledge through composition and reflection, or consolidating parametric skills via weight-space merging. Consequently, the seamless integration of text and model weights for targeted performance improvements remains largely unexplored. This work bridges this modality gap by treating model weights as an additional modality that an LLM can natively reason over. We instantiate parametric learning via prefix-tuning and augment an LLM to ingest both prefix weights and rich textual data which capture relationships to a target capability. Our augmented LLM, which we call SkillSmith, synthesizes these inputs to perform instruction-steered parametric synthesis, directly outputting new prefix weights that manifest the target skill. We demonstrate that our approach significantly outperforms both text-only and weight-space-only baselines, unlocking performance gains that are out of reach for uni-modal (text-only or weight-only) adaptations.

</details>


### [119] [Models for minimalist RAG: B1ade 335M Embedding and 1B Parameter Small Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27506)
*Shreyas Subramanian,Mecit Gungor,Vikram Elango*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出B1ade，一种高效的RAG架构，由335M参数的嵌入模型（参数无关融合五个预训练编码器）和1B参数的SLM组成。SLM通过GRPO在723M tokens上训练，仅优化答案相似度奖励，却意外涌现出42.4%的引用率（超过训练分布5.5个百分点），证明无需显式奖励工程即可实现归因行为。在QA基准和端到端RAG评估中均表现优异，缩小了与1.5倍大小模型的差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统RAG系统假设需要大规模预训练和显式grounding监督。作者旨在挑战这一假设，探索是否可以通过策略性的模型组合和奖励设计，以极低的资源成本构建高效的RAG系统，而无需依赖大规模预训练。

Method: B1ade包含两个组件：(1) B1ade-embed，335M参数检索模型，通过参数无关融合五个预训练编码器构建，零额外训练即达到顶尖MTEB分数；(2) B1ade-1B，在低预算GPU上使用GRPO（组相对策略优化）在723M tokens（2.2M示例）的精选上下文-问题对上训练，奖励仅优化答案相似度。

Result: B1ade-embed在sub-500M模型中取得顶级MTEB分数；B1ade-1B在PopQA达81.82%、PubMedQA达65.8%、FEVER达51.09%。关键发现是涌现式归因：虽无显式引用监督，模型在42.4%的响应中引用检索段落，超过训练分布5.5个百分点。端到端RAG平均得分0.654，较SFT提升10.8%。

Conclusion: 研究表明grounding行为可以在RL训练下作为精度最大化策略自然涌现，无需显式奖励工程。策略性模型组合和奖励设计足以实现资源高效的RAG，无需大规模预训练，为轻量级RAG系统提供了新范式。

Abstract: Language and embedding models used in RAG systems are conventionally assumed to require large-scale pretraining and explicit grounding supervision. We present B1ade, an efficient RAG architecture comprising two purpose-built components: a compact embedding model and a purpose-built SLM. B1ade-embed, a 335M parameter retrieval model constructed via parameter-free fusion of five pretrained encoders achieves top MTEB scores among sub-500M models with zero additional training, and B1ade-1B, an SLM trained on low-cost GPUs using Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) on 723M tokens (2.2M examples) of curated context-question pairs with rewards that optimize only answer similarity. Our central finding is emergent attribution: despite receiving no explicit supervision for source citation, B1ade-1B cites retrieved passages in 42.4% of responses, exceeding the attribution rate of its training distribution by 5.5 percentage points. This demonstrates that grounding behavior can emerge as an accuracy-maximizing strategy under RL training, without explicit reward engineering. On standard QA benchmarks, B1ade-1B achieves 81.82% on PopQA, 65.8% on PubMedQA, and 51.09% on FEVER. In end-to-end RAG evaluation, B1ade-1B achieves an average score of 0.654 across correctness, completeness, coherence, and faithfulness, a 10.8% improvement over the SFT, while closing the gap with models 1.5x its size. These results show that strategic model composition and reward design suffice for resource-efficient RAG, without large-scale pretraining.

</details>


### [120] [Belief Coevolution in a Social Network of Generalist and Specialist Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27512)
*Germans Savcisens,Samantha Dies,Courtney Maynard,Tina Eliassi-Rad*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出CoevolveSim框架，研究大规模语言模型（LLM）在多智能体网络中信念的形成与传播机制。通过1,280次受控模拟，发现角色分配和网络结构主要影响个体信念修正但对群体共识影响甚微，而引入微调的专业化LLM会使共识偏移加倍以上并产生影响力不对称性；异构LLM群体需要结合群体级信念组成与智能体身份才能准确预测信念动态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM越来越多地部署于多智能体环境中，但信念如何在交互的LLM之间形成和传播仍然缺乏理解，需要系统化的研究框架来隔离并分析影响信念扩散的关键因素。

Method: 构建CoevolveSim框架，隔离三个因素：领域专业化、社会角色分配和社会网络结构。智能体每轮观察邻居信念摘要后更新自身信念。共运行1,280次受控模拟，覆盖四种场景、两种网络结构和20条医学适应症陈述。

Result: 基于人物角色的角色分配和网络结构重塑个体信念修正但对群体共识影响甚微；引入微调的专业化LLM使共识偏移增加一倍以上并产生持续的影响力不对称性。简单基于持久性的观点动力学模型可复现全通用性LLM群体的集体结果，而异构LLM群体需要群体级信念组成和智能体身份来分别预测共识与个体信念转换。

Conclusion: 真实模拟多智能体LLM系统中的信念扩散需要多样化的底层LLM，仅靠人物角色提示（persona prompting）是不够的；异构LLM群体才能产生更接近现实社会的信念动态特征。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly deployed in multi-agent environments. However, the processes by which beliefs form and propagate among interacting LLMs remain poorly understood. We introduce CoevolveSim, a framework for studying belief diffusion within networked LLM populations. CoevolveSim allows us to isolate and study three factors: domain specialization, social-role assignment, and social network structure. Within this framework, generalist and specialist LLM agents exchange and revise beliefs. In each round, an LLM agent observes a summary of its neighbors' beliefs before updating its own. We run 1,280 controlled simulations spanning four scenarios, two network structures, and 20 medical-indication statements. We find that persona-style role assignment and network structure reshape individual belief revision but have minimal effect on population-level consensus. In contrast, introducing (finetuned) specialist LLMs more than doubles the shift in consensus and gives rise to consistent asymmetries in exerted influence. We further show that simple persistence-based opinion-dynamics models reproduce collective outcomes in all-generalist LLM populations, whereas heterogeneous LLM populations require population-level belief composition to reproduce consensus and agent identity to predict individual belief transitions. Our results indicate that realistic simulation of belief diffusion in multi-agent LLM systems requires a diverse set of underlying LLMs, not persona prompting alone.

</details>


### [121] [Training Skills Like Parameters via Self-Supervised Semantic Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27557)
*Mo Li,Zixin Yin,Ting Cao,Yunxin Liu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种无监督的自进化智能体框架，受扩散模型"破坏-重建"范式启发，通过对比智能体重建结果与人类原始作品来构建自监督信号，从而实现无需外部监督的领域技能积累。该方法在短剧编剧任务上验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型在通用指令遵循方面表现出色，但在创意编剧等高度专业化的开放领域往往不及人类专家。现有的后训练方法（如监督微调和强化学习）需要权重访问权限，且封闭源代码模型无法提供，同时计算开销大，所学内容绑定于单一检查点且无法被人类检查。已有的智能体持续学习方法依赖昂贵的人类专家标注或不可靠的LLM-as-judge反馈，存在明显瓶颈。

Method: 提出一个受扩散模型"损坏-重建"范式启发的无监督自进化智能体框架。不依赖显式外部评分，而是利用现有高质量人类作品构建自监督信号。训练遵循前向、损失、反向的标准循环，损失来自将智能体的重建结果与人类原始作品进行对比。更新的是外部文本技能库而非模型权重。

Result: 在短剧编剧这一挑战性任务上的实验表明，该框架使智能体能够自主提取和内化高度可泛化的技能，显著提升其在特定领域的生成能力。

Conclusion: 这种自对比反思范式为智能体在没有外部监督的情况下自我学习复杂高质量人类作品的生产提供了一条可扩展的路径。

Abstract: While Large Language Models (LLMs) demonstrate remarkable general instruction-following capabilities, they often fall short of human experts in highly specialized, open-ended domains such as creative screenwriting. Prior approaches typically adopt post-training, yet both supervised fine-tuning and reinforcement learning require weight access that closed-source frontier models do not offer, and demand heavy compute. Moreover, what is learned is tied to a single checkpoint and cannot be inspected by humans. Recent advancements in agentic continual learning instead attempt to bridge this gap by accumulating external textual skills. However, these methods heavily rely on costly human expert annotations or unreliable LLM-as-a-judge feedback for reflection. To overcome this bottleneck, we propose a novel, unsupervised self-evolving agent framework inspired by the corruption-and-reconstruction paradigm of diffusion models. Instead of relying on explicit external scoring, we leverage existing high-quality human artifacts to construct self-supervised signals. Training then follows the familiar loop of neural network training, forward, loss, and backward, with the loss coming from contrasting the agent's reconstruction against the human original. What is updated is not model weights but an external library of textual skills. We evaluate our framework on the challenging task of short drama screenwriting. Experimental results demonstrate that our method enables the agent to autonomously extract and internalize highly generalizable skills, significantly enhancing its domain-specific generation capabilities. Furthermore, this self-contrastive reflection paradigm offers a scalable pathway for agents to teach themselves the production of complex, high-quality human artifacts, without requiring external supervision.

</details>


### [122] [Beyond Similarity: Grounded Agentic Extraction and Expert-Adjudicated Evaluation of Intertextuality in Classical Chinese Histories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27595)
*Zhaoji Wang,Wanyu Si,Jun Wang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出将细粒度互文性提取重新构建为一个智能体（agentic）任务：让大语言模型（LLM）完整阅读两个文本单元，通过受限工具接口将每次文本复用精确锚定到两侧的字符跨度，并按照五维类型学（形式、方面、来源标记、功能、立场）进行标注。作者在《论语》与《汉书》的穷尽对比上验证了该方法，由三位领域专家裁决出包含2,533对互文对的基准数据集，并据此研究了十二个LLM的性能、成本与置信度校准。将验证后的提取器扩展到《二十四史》全文（65,380次比较，5,766对），恢复了相似度分数无法表达的语料库级结构，发现引用构成在十八个世纪中无系统性变化，但同一段落的引用越来越不字面化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的计算互文性方法（从字符串匹配到神经检索）只能输出相似度分数和平行段落列表，能识别文本何处复用，却无法刻画复用如何发生（怎样）以及为何发生（为什么）。作者希望突破这一局限，提供可解释、可标注的细粒度互文性分析框架。

Method: 将互文性提取转化为agentic任务：LLM完整阅读两个文本单元，通过受限工具接口将每次复用锚定到精确字符跨度，并按五维类型学（形式、方面、来源标记、功能、立场）标注。三位领域专家裁决多模型候选集构建基准（2,533对），研究十二个LLM的表现，并将验证后的提取器扩展到《二十四史》全文。

Result: 十二个LLM的精确率范围为56%-93%，在相当质量下存在51倍的成本差异；专家一致性沿可靠性梯度分布——文本表面可见的维度标注一致，需推断意图的维度存在争议。扩展到《二十四史》后恢复出语料库级结构：引用的解释性构成在十八个世纪中无系统性变化，但同一段落的引用越来越不字面化。

Conclusion: 智能体化LLM方法能够提供超越相似度分数的可解释互文性分析；构建的基准和提取协议揭示了个案漂移与总体稳定性并存的现象，这与文化吸引（cultural attraction）理论的预期一致。作者公开了提取协议和专家裁决的基准数据集。

Abstract: Computational approaches to intertextuality have advanced from string matching to neural retrieval, yet their outputs, similarity scores and parallel-passage lists, identify where texts reuse one another without characterizing how or why. We recast fine-grained intertextuality extraction as an agentic task in which a large language model (LLM) reads two text units in full and, through a constrained tool interface, must ground each proposed reuse in exact character spans on both sides and label it under a five-dimension typology of reuse (form, aspect, source-marking, function, stance). We validate the approach on an exhaustive comparison of the Analects with the Book of Han, where three domain experts adjudicate a pooled multi-model candidate set into a benchmark of 2,533 intertextual pairs. Against this standard we study twelve LLMs, reporting precision (56%-93%), a 51$\times$ cost spread at comparable quality, and how well their confidence is calibrated. Expert agreement traces a reliability gradient: dimensions legible on the textual surface are annotated consistently, while those requiring inference of intent are contested, delimiting the claims such annotation supports. Scaling the validated extractor to the full Twenty-Four Histories (65,380 comparisons, 5,766 pairs) recovers corpus-level structure a similarity score cannot express. The interpretive composition of citation shows no systematic change across eighteen centuries, yet the same passage is quoted less and less literally. Stability in the aggregate with drift in the individual case is what a cultural-attraction account expects. We release the extraction protocol and the expert-adjudicated benchmark.

</details>


### [123] [AWARE-FX: An Auditable Knowledge-Guided AI System for Measuring Corporate Foreign-Exchange Hedging Disclosure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27611)
*Qi Wang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了AWARE-FX，一个可审计的AI/NLP决策支持系统，用于从企业年报中提取外汇风险管理、衍生品使用、自然对冲和明确不使用等弱结构化证据，并将其转化为可追溯的公司-年度对冲披露指标。系统结合专业词表、否定和会计状态逻辑、通道专用金融编码器、精确证据门控、保守聚合和审计台账，在24,909个香港公司年度样本上检索并评分了543,527个片段。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 企业年报中包含关于外汇风险管理、衍生品使用、自然对冲和明确不使用的弱结构化证据，现有方法难以将这些文本转化为可追溯、可审计的量化指标，需要一种结合领域约束的可信决策支持系统。

Method: 系统架构包括：专业来源词表、否定与会计状态逻辑、通道特定金融编码器、精确证据门控、保守聚合策略和审计台账。通过消融实验、分层人工审计、多模型对比（FinBERT与ModernBERT）、时序测试、概率校准、选择性预测及生成模型基准进行全面评估。

Result: FinBERT在八种编码器任务比较中有七种平均F1更高，时序F1范围为0.702-0.872；对20%最不自信的时序观测进行弃权可将保留样本F1提升0.050-0.077。严格外汇评分与基线和压力期外汇敞口负相关，而通用宽泛评分则不然，提供了外部构念效度验证。

Conclusion: AWARE-FX提供了一个可测试的决策支持架构，其检索、状态逻辑、分类、不确定性处理、聚合和外部验证均保持独立可审计。通用大语言模型（如Qwen3-8B）不能均匀替代领域约束，仅在特定证据类型上表现优异。

Abstract: Corporate annual reports contain weakly structured evidence about foreign-exchange risk management, derivative use, natural hedging, and explicit non-use. This study develops AWARE-FX, an auditable AI/NLP decision-support system that converts report text into traceable firm-year hedging-disclosure measures. The system combines a professional-source lexicon, negation and accounting-status logic, channel-specific financial encoders, exact evidence gates, conservative aggregation, and an audit ledger. Across 24,909 Hong Kong firm-years from 2008-2025, it retrieves and scores 543,527 snippets. Reliability is evaluated through ablations, a stratified 300-snippet human audit, three-seed FinBERT-ModernBERT comparisons, strict 2023-2025 temporal tests, probability calibration, selective prediction, and fixed-prompt generative-model benchmarks. FinBERT has the higher mean F1 in seven of eight encoder task-split comparisons; its temporal F1 ranges from 0.702 to 0.872. Abstaining on the 20% least-confident temporal observations raises retained-sample F1 by 0.050-0.077. Deterministic Qwen3-8B performs strongly on commodity and negation evidence but poorly on foreign-debt and accounting-context labels, showing that a general-purpose LLM does not uniformly replace domain constraints. The strict FX score is negatively associated with linked baseline and stress-period FX exposure, whereas the generic broad score is not. These associations provide external construct validation, not causal estimates of hedging effectiveness. AWARE-FX contributes a tested decision-support architecture in which retrieval, status logic, classification, uncertainty handling, aggregation, and external validation remain separately auditable.

</details>


### [124] [DualAnchor: Preserving Language Priors and Improving Lexical Fidelity in Gloss-Free Sign Language Translation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27614)
*Hongbin Zhang,Junhao Liu,Xuefeng Bai,Youcheng Pan,Yang Xiang,Kehai Chen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出DualAnchor，一个无词表标注的基于大型语言模型（LLM）的手语翻译训练框架，通过Token级先验锚定（TPA）和最优传输对齐（OTA）两个互补锚点，分别解决语言先验退化（翻译不流畅）和词汇保真度缺失（词级细节不准确）两大问题，在PHOENIX-2014T和CSL-Daily数据集上取得优异性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于LLM的手语翻译方法存在两个关键缺陷：一是LLM的语言先验被削弱而非利用，导致翻译不流畅（称为"语言先验退化"）；二是现有方法通常在句子层面进行视频与文本的对齐，无法保证词级细节的准确性（称为"词汇保真度缺口"）。

Method: 提出DualAnchor框架，包含两个互补锚点：（1）Token级先验锚定（TPA）：在每个解码步骤对多模态解码器进行正则化，使其向冻结LLM在相同自回归前缀条件下的下一个词元分布对齐，从而保留语言先验；（2）最优传输对齐（OTA）：将视觉-文本匹配建模为熵正则化部分最优传输问题，通过Sinkhorn优化在余弦代价下实现视觉词元与文本内容词元之间的软对齐，提升词汇保真度。

Result: DualAnchor在PHOENIX-2014T和CSL-Daily两个基准数据集上均取得强整体性能。消融分析表明两个锚点具有互补效应：TPA提升翻译流畅性，OTA降低细粒度词级错误。

Conclusion: 本文通过双锚定机制同时解决语言流畅性和词汇保真度两个维度的问题，验证了在LLM-based手语翻译中显式保留语言先验和细粒度视觉-文本对齐的互补价值。

Abstract: Recent advances in large language models (LLMs) have led sign language translation (SLT), the task of converting sign-language videos into spoken-language text, to increasingly adopt LLMs as textual backbones. However, despite their strong language modeling capabilities, existing LLM-based SLT methods often undermine rather than exploit this language prior, producing disfluent translations, a failure we term language-prior degradation. Meanwhile, existing methods typically align videos and text at the sentence level, which does not ensure accurate lexical details and creates a lexical fidelity gap. To address both issues, we propose DualAnchor, a gloss-free LLM-based SLT training framework that couples two complementary anchors for linguistically fluent and visually faithful generation. Token-level Prior Anchoring (TPA) preserves the LLM's language prior by regularizing the multimodal decoder at each decoding step toward the next-token distribution of a frozen LLM conditioned on the same autoregressive prefix. Optimal Transport Alignment (OTA) improves lexical fidelity by formulating visual-textual matching as entropy-regularized partial optimal transport, with Sinkhorn optimization inducing a soft alignment between visual tokens and textual content tokens under a cosine cost. DualAnchor achieves strong overall performance on both PHOENIX-2014T and CSL-Daily. Targeted analyses attribute these gains to the complementary effects of the two anchors: TPA improves fluency, whereas OTA reduces fine-grained lexical errors.

</details>


### [125] [Harness-G: A Graph-Structured Harness for Search Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27652)
*Yanning Hou,Haoyuan Chen,Sihang Zhou,Xiaoshu Chen,Xirui Liu,Duanyang Yuan,Lingyuan Meng,Quan Liu,Jian Huang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出Harness-G，一种图结构检索框架，将强化学习中自由形式的查询生成重构为有限动作选择，解决检索等同崩塌（retrieval-equivalence collapse）问题，并引入结构化非短视信用分配（SNC）方法，在六个QA基准上优于最强基线Graph-R1达10.74（1.5B）和3.98（3B）个F1点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RL检索代理通常将检索建模为自由形式的自然语言查询生成，并用最终答案奖励优化多轮交互。已有研究主要关注更稠密或更结构化的信用信号，但很少审视策略-环境接口层面的检索建模是否恰当。作者观察到Search-R1训练中存在显著的检索混叠现象：同一问题的回滚持续生成不同的查询字符串，但累积的证据集越来越重叠，即"检索等价崩塌"，导致同一组内的回报缺乏有效的检索对比度。

Method: 提出Harness-G框架，将自由形式查询生成重构为有限动作选择：策略选择证据句子或实体，或选择回答，而环境负责构建选项菜单、跟踪检索状态、验证并执行每个选择。该接口减少语言混叠，使同状态替代方案可直接比较。在此基础上引入结构化非短视信用分配（SNC），使用冻结的答案评分器比较所选动作与其替代方案，将下游收益分配给使其成为可能的早期动作。

Result: 在六个QA基准上，Harness-G在两个评估模型规模下均取得最高平均F1，在1.5B规模超过最强基线Graph-R1达10.74个F1点，在3B规模超过3.98个F1点。

Conclusion: 通过重新设计策略-环境接口，将检索从自由文本生成转为图结构上的有限动作选择，有效缓解了检索等价崩塌问题，并配合SNC信用分配方法显著提升了多轮检索问答的性能。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) search agents commonly model retrieval as free-form natural-language query generation and optimize multi-turn interactions using final-answer rewards. Current studies mainly improve training with denser or more structured credit signals, but rarely examine whether retrieval is properly formulated at the policy-environment interface. We observe pronounced retrieval aliasing during Search-R1 training: rollouts for the same question continue to generate distinct query strings, yet their accumulated evidence sets increasingly overlap. We call this phenomenon retrieval-equivalence collapse; in this regime, trajectories approach utility equivalence with respect to retrieval decisions, leaving within-group returns with little effective retrieval contrast. To address this problem, we propose Harness-G, a graph-structured retrieval framework that redesigns this interface. It reformulates free-form query generation as finite action selection: the policy selects an evidence sentence or entity, or chooses to answer, while the environment constructs the menu, tracks retrieval state, and validates and executes each choice. This interface reduces linguistic aliasing and makes same-state alternatives directly comparable. Building on this interface, we introduce Structured Non-myopic Credit (SNC), which uses a frozen answer scorer to compare the selected action with its alternatives and assigns downstream gains to the earlier actions that enabled them. Across six QA benchmarks, Harness-G achieves the highest average F1 at both evaluated model scales, outperforming the strongest baseline, Graph-R1, by 10.74 points at 1.5B and 3.98 points at 3B.

</details>


### [126] [From Single- to Cross-Document: Benchmarking Multi-Granularity Event Analysis of Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27654)
*Tao Wen,Shuai Shao,Pei Ke,Xu Han,Jie Zou,Guannan Li,Tao Tian,Jinjie Qiu,Lan Wang,Ke Qin*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了MiGUE-Bench，一个系统性的多粒度事件分析评测基准，并配套开发了LLM驱动的自校正标注框架MiGUE-Pipeline，通过四个核心任务（事件检测、关系推理、结构归纳和未来预测）全面评估LLMs在事件分析上的能力边界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有事件分析评测基准存在文档粒度受限、任务设计单一和数据来源局限等问题，导致对LLMs在事件分析领域的综合能力缺乏全面理解。

Method: 开发了MiGUE-Pipeline（LLM驱动的自校正标注框架）以支持大规模高质量事件数据获取；设计了四个核心任务（事件检测、关系推理、结构归纳、未来预测）分别评估从原子事件细节到跨文档复杂叙事的模型能力。

Result: 通过对最先进的LLMs和检索增强生成（RAG）方法的大量实验，描绘了当前模型的能力边界，并识别出关键缺陷。

Conclusion: MiGUE-Bench为LLMs在挑战性事件分析任务上的未来改进提供了洞见和方向。

Abstract: Event analysis is an essential and fundamental direction of information extraction, involving various event-centric tasks at different granularity of documents. While large language models (LLMs) have preliminarily achieved promising performance in part of these tasks individually, their capability in event analysis still lacks comprehensive understanding due to restricted document granularity, task designs, and data source of existing benchmarks. To address these limitations, we introduce MiGUE-Bench, a systematic benchmark for assessing the performance of LLMs in multi-granularity event analysis. To support large-scale evaluation, we first develop an LLM-driven self-correcting annotation framework called MiGUE-Pipeline, enabling scalable acquisition of high-quality source data of events with automatic labels. Then, we design four core tasks in our benchmark, i.e., event detection, relation reasoning, structure induction, and future prediction, to probe model competence at different levels, from atomic event details to complex cross-document narratives. Extensive experiments on state-of-the-art LLMs and retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) methods delineate the current capability boundary and identify critical deficiencies, providing insights into the future improvement of LLMs in challenging event analysis tasks.

</details>


### [127] [ICLE++: Modeling Fine-Grained Traits for Holistic Essay Scoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27671)
*Shengjie Li,Vincent Ng*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文介绍了ICLE++，一个包含整体评分和特质评分的学生议论文语料库，用于测试AES模型在ASAP语料库之外的泛化能力，并支持多特质评分和跨提示评分等新兴AES问题的评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目前大多数自动作文评分（AES）模型仅在ASAP语料库上评估，但ASAP存在局限性，无法确定模型在其他语料库上的泛化能力，因此需要新的标注语料库来解决这一问题。

Method: 构建ICLE++语料库，对ICLE语料库中的学生议论文进行长期标注，同时提供整体评分和特质评分，支持多特质评分和跨提示评分等AES任务的评估。

Result: ICLE++作为新的标注语料库，可用于测试AES模型在ASAP之外的泛化性能，并支持新兴AES任务（多特质评分和跨提示评分）的评估。

Conclusion: ICLE++是长期标注工作的重要成果，为AES研究提供了亟需的标注语料库资源，有助于推动AES模型的泛化性测试和新型评分任务的发展。

Abstract: The majority of the recently-developed models for automated essay scoring (AES) are evaluated solely on the ASAP corpus. However, ASAP is not without its limitations. For instance, it is not clear whether models trained on ASAP can generalize well when evaluated on other corpora. In light of these limitations, we introduce ICLE++, a corpus of persuasive student essays annotated with both holistic scores and trait-specific scores. Not only can ICLE++ be used to test the generalizability of AES models trained on ASAP, but it can also facilitate the evaluation of models developed for newer AES problems such as multi-trait scoring and cross-prompt scoring. We believe that ICLE++, which represents a culmination of our long-term effort in annotating the essays in the ICLE corpus, contributes to the set of much-needed annotated corpora for AES research.

</details>


### [128] [Recall Before You Rank: Similarity-Guided Top-$K$ Reuse for Efficient Long-Context Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27692)
*Wenshuai Yao,Wenyong Zhou,Hanyong Shao,Yizhe Chen,Zhiyuan Ning,Yuannuo Feng,Ru Huang,Kechao Tang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: ReTopK是一种免训练的动态Top-K稀疏注意力加速方法，通过复用历史查询-支持对的检索决策，在保持接近精确Top-K质量的同时显著加速长上下文注意力计算。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Top-K稀疏注意力虽能降低Softmax和值聚合成本，但选择Top-K子集仍需对完整KV缓存打分并做全局选择，选择器开销随上下文长度线性增长，限制了长上下文解码中的实际效率。

Method: ReTopK维护每个注意力头的有界历史查询-支持对缓存，对新查询检索最相似的历史查询，将其存储的支持集与近期窗口取并集，仅对紧凑候选集用精确当前查询分数重排序。采用基于相似度的回退机制在复用不可靠时调用全历史精确Top-K，并通过周期性精确刷新限制缓存漂移。只复用选择索引而非历史分数或输出。

Result: 在16K-128K上下文中，ReTopK在PG19困惑度、NIAH和LongBench评分上均取得最优。128K上下文且K=512时，相比精确Top-K仅增加0.50%困惑度，同时加速注意力计算3.07倍。

Conclusion: 通过复用历史检索决策，ReTopK在不牺牲质量的前提下显著提升了动态Top-K稀疏注意力在长上下文场景中的实际效率，为长上下文解码提供了一种有效的免训练加速方案。

Abstract: Top-$K$ sparse attention reduces the cost of Softmax and value aggregation by attending to only a small subset of key--value (KV) entries. However, identifying this subset still requires scoring the current query against the full KV cache and performing global Top-$K$ selection, leaving selector cost linear in context length and limiting the practical efficiency of sparse attention for long-context decoding. In this paper, we introduce ReTopK, a training-free method that accelerates dynamic Top-$K$ attention by reusing historical retrieval decisions. ReTopK builds on the observation that similar queries often attend to overlapping supports and that partially overlapping supports can still preserve most of the Exact Top-$K$ attention mass. For each attention head, it maintains a bounded cache of historical query--support pairs, retrieves the most similar cached queries for each new query, unions their stored supports with a recent window, and reranks only the resulting compact candidate set using exact current-query scores. A similarity-based fallback invokes full-history Exact Top-$K$ when reuse is unreliable, while periodic exact refreshes limit cache drift. ReTopK retains the complete KV cache and reuses only selected indices, rather than historical scores, attention weights, or outputs. Across 16K--128K contexts, ReTopK achieves the lowest PG19 perplexity and the highest NIAH and LongBench scores among the evaluated approximate methods. At 128K with $K=512$, ReTopK incurs only a 0.50\% perplexity increase over Exact Top-$K$ while accelerating attention computation by $3.07\times$.

</details>


### [129] [A Sparse Glimpse of the Whole: Train-Free Self-Speculative Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27735)
*Yuesong Liu,Yuan Zeng,Min Lyu,Ruilin Liu,Yu Guo,Yinlong Xu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出SparseSpec-L，一个无需训练的自我投机解码框架，用于长上下文推理加速。它通过动态稀疏化和可召回KV缓存生成轻量级草稿，利用注意力统计作为重要性信号，并通过在线熵控制器自适应选择投机长度，实现高达数倍端到端加速且不改变输出分布。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 投机解码虽能缓解大语言模型推理中的内存带宽瓶颈，但其加速效果受起草开销、令牌接受率和投机长度三方面共同制约。现有方法未系统分析投机长度扩展对加速的影响，且长上下文场景下如何高效生成草稿仍是挑战。

Method: 1) 提出统一效率分析框架，证明当边际接受概率低于相对起草成本时，延长投机视野反而降低加速比。2) 提出SparseSpec-L框架：利用动态稀疏化和可召回KV缓存直接从目标模型生成轻量级草稿；回收全上下文验证阶段产生的逐头注意力统计作为无额外前向的重要性信号，实现关键历史令牌召回而不永久丢弃密集KV缓存；采用在线基于熵的控制器根据期望逐步效率自适应选择投机长度。

Result: 在多项长上下文任务和多个模型规模上，SparseSpec-L相比自回归解码实现了一致的端到端加速，最高可达数倍加速比，同时保持目标模型的输出分布不变。

Conclusion: SparseSpec-L通过理论分析指导设计，证明在长上下文推理中通过自适应投机长度控制和可召回KV缓存策略，可以在无需训练的前提下实现显著的推理加速，且不牺牲输出质量。

Abstract: Speculative decoding alleviates the memory-bandwidth bottleneck in large language model inference, but its acceleration is jointly constrained by drafting overhead, token acceptance, and speculation length. We present a unified efficiency analysis showing that extending the speculation horizon can reduce rather than improve speedup when the marginal acceptance probability falls below the relative drafting cost. Guided by this analysis, we introduce SparseSpec-L, a training-free self-speculative decoding framework for long-context inference. SparseSpec-L generates lightweight drafts directly from the target model using a dynamically sparsified and recallable KV cache. It recycles per-head attention statistics produced during full-context verification as a no-extra-forward importance signal, allowing critical historical tokens to be recalled without permanently discarding the dense KV cache. An online entropy-based controller further selects the speculation length according to expected step-wise efficiency. Experiments across multiple long-context tasks and model scales show consistent end-to-end acceleration, with up to speedup over autoregressive decoding while preserving the target model's output distribution.

</details>


### [130] [Can LVLMs Uncover the Truth Behind Visual Illusions? An Analysis of Perceptual and Reasoning Capabilities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27747)
*Liangjie Zhao,Jiaqing Lyu,Kexin Tang,Zecheng Fang,Rong Yin,Yulan Hu,Da Li,Jianing Li*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了IllusionReasoning基准，利用视觉错觉图像来评测大型视觉语言模型（LVLMs）的联合感知与推理能力，发现现有LVLMs的推理能力并不如宣称的那样先进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LVLM评测要么只关注感知能力，要么局限于数学或编程等特定领域，缺乏在开放世界环境中联合考虑感知与推理的评估方法。

Method: 利用视觉错觉作为诊断工具，构建了从真实世界收集的错觉图像基准IllusionReasoning，并配以多样化的标注问答对来评测LVLMs。

Result: 实验表明，广泛范围内的LVLMs的推理能力并不像宣称的那样先进。

Conclusion: 该工作为LVLMs提供了新见解，并为未来优化方向提供了指引。

Abstract: Large Vision Language Models have integrated reasoning capabilities, elevating cognitive performance to new levels. However, existing evaluations either focus solely on perception or rely on specific domains such as maths or coding. Evaluation for reasoning capabilities that align with an open-world environment is still required, especially one that considers perception and reasoning jointly. To bridge this gap, we propose to evaluate LVLMs by exploiting visual illusions as a diagnostic tool. Visual illusions are phenomena in which the human visual system misinterprets objective signals, resulting in an understanding that deviates from reality. We constructed IllusionReasoning, a benchmark of illusion images collected from the real world, incorporating diverse annotated question-answer pairs. Based on IllusionReasoning, we show that the reasoning capabilities of a wide range of LVLMs are not as advanced as claimed. Our work provides new insights into LVLMs and offers future direction for optimisation.

</details>


### [131] [Gradient-free Task-Conditioned Retrieval for On-Device In-Context Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27766)
*Xinyu Luo,Hui Liu,Yihua Shao,Junyi Yang,Arindam Basu,Haoliang Li*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: CoRA是一种无需梯度训练的框架，通过封闭形式的岭回归将冻结编码器转换为任务条件检索器，实现设备端上下文学习的高效示范检索，无需微调或反向传播。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 设备端上下文学习依赖推理前的检索来选择示范，但现有方法需要在有限的计算、内存和数据暴露预算下同时利用任务特定信息，这是一个挑战。

Method: CoRA框架包含：选择互补编码器层，从候选记忆构建输出推导的条件空间，通过封闭形式岭回归对齐候选输入表示，使用低秩分解生成紧凑检索基。支持精确的两遍流式构建以避免物化完整拟合矩阵，并扩展到多模态示范检索。

Result: 在十个文本数据集和四个多模态基准上，使用Llama-3.2-1B、MobileLLM-Pro、OpenFlamingo-3B和Qwen3.5-2B进行实验，并在Raspberry Pi 5上端到端部署，证明CoRA无需检索器微调、反向传播或目标模型调用即可实现有效的任务条件检索。

Conclusion: CoRA证明了设备端任务条件检索可以通过无梯度、低秩的方法实现，满足严格的计算和内存约束，并在文本和多模态场景中均有效。

Abstract: On-device in-context learning (ICL) relies on pre-inference retrieval to select demonstrations for useful context before downstream model inference. This retrieval must exploit task-specific information while operating over local memories under limited computation, memory, and data-exposure budgets. We propose Conditional Retrieval Alignment (CoRA), a gradient-free framework that converts a frozen encoder into a task-conditioned retriever using paired candidate inputs and outputs. CoRA selects complementary encoder layers, constructs an output-derived conditioning space from candidate memory, and aligns candidate input representations to this space through closed-form ridge regression. Low-rank factorization then produces a compact retrieval basis where candidate outputs are used only during offline index construction, whereas query-time retrieval requires only the query input and precomputed index. We show that CoRA's rank-constrained basis is the optimal low-rank compression of the output-conditioned fitted representation, and derive an exact two-pass streaming construction that avoids materializing the full fitted matrix. We further extend the framework to multimodal exemplar retrieval by incorporating visual representations into the conditioning and retrieval spaces. Experiments across ten textual datasets and four multimodal benchmarks with Llama-3.2-1B, MobileLLM-Pro, OpenFlamingo-3B, and Qwen3.5-2B, as well as end-to-end Raspberry Pi~5 deployment demonstrate that CoRA supports effective task-conditioned retrieval without retriever fine-tuning, backpropagation, or target-model calls.

</details>


### [132] [ChronoMem: Version Control and Semantic Rollback for Large Language Model Agent Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27773)
*Yongye Su,Wujiang Xu,Chaoji Zuo,Elisa Bertino*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出ChronoMem，一个为LLM代理设计的语义版本控制层，通过在每个记忆写入时提交全量快照、维护结构化版本历史，并支持自然语言回滚请求，实现了代理记忆的系统性回滚能力。作者还引入了一种"暴露后评估协议"，在长时对话基准上验证了ChronoMem相较于提示式和检索式基线的显著优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM代理的记忆系统仅支持前向演化（持续累积、整合、覆盖知识），缺乏检查、版本管理或回滚先前状态的机制，导致代理在遇到修正、概念漂移或记忆损坏时表现脆弱，尤其在已接触后续信息的情况下问题更为突出。

Method: ChronoMem作为语义版本控制层集成到Google的生产级开源Agent Development Kit中。其核心方法包括：(1) 每次记忆写入时提交全量记忆快照；(2) 维护结构化版本历史；(3) 通过混合词汇与语义检索、排序融合和重排序，将自然语言回滚意图映射到具体历史版本。同时引入"暴露后评估协议"来测试代理在回滚后的反事实行为表现。

Result: 在引入演化记忆状态和回滚任务的长时对话基准上，ChronoMem在回滚一致性问答和历史总结方面显著优于仅提示和仅检索的基线方法，并在语义版本选择上展现出强性能。

Conclusion: ChronoMem是首个面向LLM代理的系统性语义全局记忆回滚的开源系统和基准，填补了代理记忆管理中版本控制与回滚能力的空白。

Abstract: LLM agents increasingly rely on long-term memory to support multi-session interaction and personalization. However, existing agent memory systems are designed around forward-only evolution, continuously accumulating, consolidating, and overwriting knowledge, with no principled mechanism to inspect, version, or revert prior states. This makes agents brittle under corrections, concept drift, and memory corruption, particularly after they have already been exposed to subsequent information. We present ChronoMem, a semantic version-control layer for agentic memory integrated into the production-ready, open-source Agent Development Kit by Google. ChronoMem commits whole-memory snapshots at each memory write, maintains structured version histories, and supports natural-language rollback requests by mapping undo intents to concrete historical versions through hybrid lexical and semantic retrieval, rank fusion, and reranking. We further introduce a post-exposure evaluation protocol that tests whether an agent can behave counterfactually after rollback by answering queries and summarizing history as if future updates had never occurred. On long-horizon conversational benchmarks augmented with evolving memory states and rollback tasks, ChronoMem substantially improves rollback-consistent question answering and history summarization relative to prompt-only and retrieval-only baselines, while achieving strong performance in semantic version selection. To our knowledge, ChronoMem is the first open-source system and benchmark for systematic semantic global memory rollback in LLM agents.

</details>


### [133] [Reasoning Consensus: Structural Ensembling of LLM Reasoning via Weighted DAG Aggregation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27783)
*Amruta Parulekar,Jinu Lee,Dilek Hakkani-Tür,Hari Sundaram*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种通过加权合并多个大语言模型推理链中提取的有向无环图（DAG）来集成推理结构的方法，而非仅集成答案。每个推理步骤根据多少条推理轨迹独立支持它来加权，从而生成"共识推理"图。在六个基准测试中，该方法在多数投票基线上取得了最高3.1%的准确率提升，同时提供了可检查的推理图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型通过思维链探索问题，但这种探索隐藏在非结构化的文本中。在高风险任务中，用户无法判断哪些步骤有充分依据、哪些替代方案被认真考虑过、最终结论与被模型丢弃的方案相比如何。现有的方法只集成答案而不集成推理结构，缺乏透明度和可解释性。

Method: 从多个LLM的推理链中提取有向无环图（DAG），将多个推理链的DAG结构进行加权合并。每个推理步骤根据多少条推理轨迹独立支持它来赋予权重，形成"共识推理"图。该方法允许在相同轨迹预算下与自洽性方法进行对比。

Result: 在六个基准测试（涵盖法理解释、研究生级科学、叙事多跳推理和一阶逻辑）中，该方法优于匹配预算的多数投票基线，在MuSR-MM上最大准确率提升3.1%。在单一模型上，以相同轨迹预算达到或超过自洽性方法的性能。共识子图在54.4-65.4%的头对头比较中被优先选择（五个数据集中的六个），集成权重与LLM裁判对推理质量的排名相关性为Spearman ρ=0.30-0.51。

Conclusion: 该框架不仅提升了推理任务的准确性，还提供了可检查、透明的共识推理图，增强了用户对推理过程和结论的信任与理解。此外，该框架还可用于分析同一问题的多种推理视角，具有广泛的应用价值。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) explore problems through chain-of-thought, but this exploration is buried in unstructured prose. On high-stakes tasks, users cannot tell which steps are well-supported, which alternatives were seriously considered, or how the final conclusion compares to those the model discarded. We propose a framework that ensembles the reasoning structure, not just the answers, of multiple LLMs by weighted merging of Directed Acyclic Graphs (DAGs) extracted from reasoning chains. We weight each step by how many traces independently attest to it, to return "Consensus Reasoning". Across six benchmarks spanning statutory interpretation, graduate-level science, narrative multi-hop reasoning, and first-order logic, our ensemble outperforms a matched-budget majority-vote baseline, with a maximum accuracy gain of 3.1% on MuSR-MM (narrative multi-hop reasoning). On a single model, the framework matches or exceeds self-consistency at the same trace budget while additionally exposing an inspectable consensus reasoning graph. Ensemble weights correlate with LLM-judge rankings of reasoning quality at Spearman $ρ= 0.30$-$0.51$, and consensus subgraphs are preferred over alternatives leading to the majority-vote answer in 54.4-65.4% of head-to-head comparisons across five of six datasets. We observe that our framework can also be used to analyze diverse reasoning perspectives for a problem.

</details>


### [134] [Semantic-Aligned Structural Abstraction for Multimodal Sentiment Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27790)
*Wei Chen,Junkai Li,Tongguan Wang,Hui Liu,Feiyue Xue,Chuanxiang Ma,Ying Sha*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出SentiLLM框架，通过语义对齐的结构抽象将非语言模态信号转化为紧凑的语义标记，并利用双流显著性-上下文校准机制将情感显著变化与背景状态进行校准，使LLM能够理解复杂的多模态情感序列，在四个数据集上取得SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于LLM的多模态情感分析方法主要捕获低层表面特征，未能建模由结构变化和上下文交互产生的情感语义，忽视了非语言模态与自然语言之间的结构同构性。

Method: 提出SentiLLM框架，包含两个核心组件：(1) 语义对齐的结构抽象，将连续原始信号蒸馏为紧凑且有语义意义的标记；(2) 双流显著性-上下文校准机制，将非语言特征序列分解为焦点流（捕获由文本先验引导的情感显著变化）和环境流（表征稳定背景状态），通过校准动态情感变化与背景状态，将非语言模态投影到统一语义空间。

Result: 在MOSI、MOSEI、CH-SIMS和CH-SIMS v2四个数据集上取得优越性能，且仅需少量可训练参数即可显著提升判别性能。

Conclusion: SentiLLM验证了结构抽象范式在多模态情感分析中的有效性，作为即插即用模块，能有效提升LLM对非语言情感序列的理解能力。

Abstract: Multimodal Sentiment Analysis (MSA) aims to interpret complex human emotions by integrating natural language with non-verbal modalities. Non-verbal modalities share a structural isomorphism with natural language, as both can be viewed as feature sequences evolving over time. This isomorphism enables the transformation of non-verbal modalities into text-like tokens for unified semantic reasoning. Large Language Models (LLMs), designed to understand and generate sequential data, can thus be utilized to interpret complex affective sequences. However, existing LLM-based methods primarily capture low-level superficial features, failing to model affective semantics arising from structural variations and contextual interactions. To address this limitation, we propose \textbf{SentiLLM}, a unified framework that leverages \textit{Semantic-Aligned Structural Abstraction} to distill continuous raw signals into compact, semantically meaningful tokens. Specifically, we introduce a \textit{Dual-Stream Salience-Context Calibration Mechanism}, which disentangles non-verbal feature sequences into a focus stream and an ambient stream. The focus stream captures salient sentiment shifts (e.g., facial expressions) guided by textual priors, while the ambient stream characterizes stable background states. Through calibrating these dynamic sentiment shifts against background states, SentiLLM effectively projects non-verbal modalities into a unified semantic space, making them naturally understandable for LLMs. Serving as a plug-and-play module, SentiLLM significantly improves discriminative performance with only a small number of trainable parameters. Our method achieves superior performance on four datasets, MOSI, MOSEI, CH-SIMS, and CH-SIMS v2, demonstrating the effectiveness of the structural abstraction paradigm in MSA. Our code is available at: \href{https://github.com/especiallyW/SentiLLM}.

</details>


### [135] [Beyond Borrowed Histories: Person-Aligned User Simulation for Interactive Role-Playing Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27816)
*Yuhang Zhu,Mingxuan Du,Benfeng Xu,Jie Gao,Lingyun Yu,Hongtao Xie*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出PALATE基准，基于用户模拟器对角色扮演智能体（RPA）进行可扩展评估，克服传统固定对话历史和固定评分标准的问题，通过个性化评分标准更好地与真实用户满意度对齐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RPA评估基准存在两个局限：一是要求RPA在固定对话历史基础上继续生成，无法科学评估其真实多轮对话中的角色扮演能力；二是用户满意度因人而异，固定评分标准与用户实际体验不一致。

Method: PALATE构建300个角色档案池，训练五个针对不同用户的模拟器，让它们与候选RPA进行自由形式的多轮对话。除一般质量评分标准外，还构建个性化评分标准以衡量用户满意度，并在主评估中对16个候选系统分别刻画通用轮次质量、长会话能力和个性化用户体验。

Result: 在人工标注数据上，个性化评分标准比一般评分标准与人类判断的一致性更高；主评估中PALATE能对特定用户-RPA组合产生可解释的评估结果，而非单一的用户无关排名。

Conclusion: PALATE通过用户模拟器和个人化评估框架，为RPA提供了更贴近真实用户满意度的评估方法，能够产生更细致、可解释的评估结果。

Abstract: Role-playing agents (RPAs) have become one of the most important consumer applications of large language models. Users engage in multi-turn conversations with RPAs for experiences such as emotional comfort, making reliable evaluation essential for measuring capability, comparing systems, and guiding further improvement. Existing benchmarks, however, typically require an RPA to continue a fixed dialogue history and then evaluate the continuation using a fixed rubric detached from the user. We identify and empirically demonstrate two limitations of this design. First, an RPA's output is shaped by the preceding dialogue history, preventing a scientifically grounded assessment of its role-playing ability in real multi-turn settings. Second, user experience varies substantially across individuals, and conventional fixed rubrics need not align with user satisfaction. We therefore introduce PALATE (Person-Aligned LLM-Simulated-User Assessment with Tailored Evaluation), a scalable RPA benchmark built on user simulators. PALATE is accompanied by a pool of 300 character profiles. Its main evaluation trains five per-user simulators and lets them engage candidate RPAs in free-form, multi-turn conversations over a pre-frozen panel of character profiles. Alongside a general quality rubric, we construct personalized rubrics to measure user satisfaction; on held-out annotated data, the personalized rubrics show higher agreement with human judgments than the general rubric.
